info: LidoSRv3/Audit/Verity/SszTxSimulation.lean:346:0: LidoSRv3.Audit.Verity.SszTxSimulation.ssz_tx_simulation_correct {State : Type} (input : TxInputs) (before after : State)
  (attempts : List CallAttempt) (trace : CommitTrace) (hwidths : exactTxWidths input) :
  exactTxWidths input ∧
    (digestPreimages input.toInputs).length = 7 ∧
      (runVerification input = VerificationOutcome.accept ↔ computedRoot input = input.expectedDepositDataRoot) ∧
        (runVerification input = VerificationOutcome.revertRootMismatch →
          (transactionObservation input before after attempts trace).committedState = before)

  (state : CallState) : (DenoteExternalCalls.denote (sha256Calls input) adversary state).snd.world = state.world
info: LidoSRv3/Audit/Verity/SszTxSimulation.lean:348:0: Compiler.CompilationModel.Denote.Sha256.denote_success_compose (oracle : StaticCallOracle)
  (fipsSha256 : ByteArray → ByteArray) (world : Verity.ContractState) (inputOffset inputSize outputOffset : Nat)
  (digest : Digest) (sha256_correct : sha256_correct oracle fipsSha256)
  (hcall : oracle (request world.memory inputOffset inputSize outputOffset) = StaticCallResult.success digest) :
  Denote.Sha256.denote oracle world inputOffset inputSize outputOffset =
